📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期猴痘病毒(Mpox病毒)研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 深度学习图像分类器在诊断文献中占主导地位,其报道的区分 猴痘 皮肤病变与临床模拟的准确度和曲线下面积值范围约为 0.83 至 0.99。时间序列和神经网络模型(ARIMA、LSTM、GRU 和集成亚流行框架)产生了可靠的短期病例预测,而自然语言处理管道则近乎实时地跟踪公众情绪和错误信息。人工智能辅助的 基因组监测、废水监测以及基于结构的药物和表位发现代表了快速新兴但不太成熟的领域。
  • 系统发育分析证实所有泰国分离株均为 Ib 分支,与国际 Ib 分支病毒密切相关,与 Ia 分支参考相比,C3L 基因区域有 1,140 bp 缺失和 52-61 个氨基酸取代。 [10.1128/spectrum.00516-26]。
  • Ib 和 IIb.A.2.1 谱系的感染表明,两种猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 变种都会引起皮肤和睾丸病变,以及鼻腔、皮肤和睾丸组织的病毒排泄。 [10.1080/21505594.2026.2728484]。
  • 我们研究了不同的 疫苗接种 策略如何影响分叉方向,并分析了各种初始暴露和感染人群水平的相应吸引盆地。 [10.1038/s41598-026-72308-1]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Deep-learning image classifiers dominated the diagnostic literature, with reported accuracies and area-under-the-curve values ranging from approximately 0.83 to 0.99 for distinguishing mpox skin lesions from clinical mimics.
  • Phylogenetic analysis confirmed all Thai isolates as clade Ib, showing close relatedness to international clade Ib viruses and exhibiting a 1,140-bp deletion in the C3L gene region and 52–61 amino acid substitutions compared with the clade Ia reference.
  • Among the 1,162 reported mpox cases, 776 (66.8%) were from large urban areas. [10.1186/s12982-026-02649-9].
  • Infection with the Clade Ib and IIb.A.2.1 lineages revealed that both Monkeypox virus (MPXV) variants caused skin and testicular lesions, as well as viral excretion from the nasal cavity, skin, and testicular tissue.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Microbiology Spectrum  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-24  |  DOI: 10.1128/spectrum.00516-26  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0051626

泰国前 5 例猴痘病毒 Ib 分支病例的基因组特征:2024 年 8 月至 2025 年 3 月

Genomic characteristics of the first five monkeypox virus clade Ib cases in Thailand: August 2024–March 2025
作者： Ravee Nitiyanontakij, Sarinee Reawrang, Duangkamon Siludjai, Pornphimon Kaewsawang, Pimphorn Yooma, Ratchana Ponang, Pawita Suwanvattana, Peera Hemarajata, Wittawin Worakitchanon, Supaporn Wacharapluesadee
摘要中文翻译摘要 2024 年 8 月刚果民主共和国出现猴痘病毒 Ib 分支后,由于 传播 的持续传播和国际传播,世界卫生组织宣布该病毒为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件。我们对 2024 年 8 月至 2025 年 3 月期间在泰国发现的第一批 Ib 分支病例进行了特征分析,并评估了它们的系统发育关系。对疑似病例的病变拭子进行全 基因组 测序。系统发育分析,结合来自 GISAID 的进化枝 Ia (DQ011155.1) 和全局进化枝 Ib 序列,确认所有泰国分离株均为进化枝 Ib。基因组 显示与国际报道的 Ib 分支病毒密切相关,与患者的旅行史一致。与进化枝 Ia 参考相比,所有分离株在 C3L 基因区域均表现出 1,140 bp 缺失和 52-61 个氨基酸取代。基因组 序列已提交给 GISAID 和 NCBI,以支持 暴发 响应并提高对流行病学模式的理解,从而实现更有效的公共卫生干预措施。重要性 2024 年 8 月 14 日,世界卫生组织宣布非洲猴痘病毒 暴发 为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件,继出现 Ib 分支后。一周后,泰国确认亚洲首例猴痘分支Ib病例。通过对 2024 年 8 月至 2025 年 3 月期间检测到的 5 个病例进行全 基因组 测序和系统发育分析,我们在 C3L 基因区域中发现了一个 1,140 bp 的缺失,该缺失将分支 Ib 与分支 Ia 区分开来。这种缺失直接影响了一些为进化枝 Ia 设计的商业实时 PCR 检测的性能,这些检测依赖于缺失基因组区域的扩增。我们的研究结果强调了重要的诊断局限性,这些局限性可能导致常规检测中的误解或敏感性降低。除了确认区域引入与全球传播相关之外,这项研究还强调了 基因组监测 在确保准确的 诊断、监测病毒 进化 以及随着猴痘继续在国际上蔓延而加强 暴发 应对方面的关键作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
需要开展这项研究来了解猴痘病毒 Ib 分支在泰国的出现及其对公共卫生的影响,因为由于 传播 的持续存在以及 2024 年 8 月在刚果民主共和国出现后的国际传播,世界卫生组织宣布该病毒为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件。
设计与对象
该研究描述了 2024 年 8 月至 2025 年 3 月期间在泰国发现的前 5 例猴痘病毒 Ib 分支病例,评估了它们与 GISAID 的全球 Ib 分支序列和 Ia 分支参考序列的系统发育关系。
核心方法
对疑似病例的病变拭子进行全 基因组 测序,并进行系统发育分析,将泰国分离株与 Ia 分支和 Ib 分支序列进行比较,鉴定出 C3L 基因区域的 1,140 bp 缺失和氨基酸替换。
主要结果
系统发育分析证实所有泰国分离株均为 Ib 分支,与国际 Ib 分支病毒密切相关,与 Ia 分支参考相比,C3L 基因区域有 1,140 bp 缺失和 52-61 个氨基酸取代。
解释与创新
研究结果证实了 Ib 分支猴痘病毒的区域引入与全球传播有关,并强调了 基因组监测 在监测病毒 进化 和提高诊断准确性方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
随着猴痘继续在国际上传播,该研究为加强诊断测试、了解 传播 动态以及为公共卫生干预措施提供重要见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于五个病例,并依赖于基因组数据,这可能无法完全捕捉流行病学模式,但由于使用了全球参考序列和泰国分离株的直接测序,证据是强有力的。

Genomic characteristics of the first five monkeypox virus clade Ib cases in Thailand: August 2024–March 2025

Authors: Ravee Nitiyanontakij, Sarinee Reawrang, Duangkamon Siludjai, Pornphimon Kaewsawang, Pimphorn Yooma, Ratchana Ponang, Pawita Suwanvattana, Peera Hemarajata, Wittawin Worakitchanon, Supaporn Wacharapluesadee
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Following the emergence of monkeypox virus clade Ib in the Democratic Republic of the Congo in August 2024, the World Health Organization declared a public health emergency of international concern because of sustained transmission and international spread. We characterized the first clade Ib cases detected in Thailand between August 2024 and March 2025 and assessed their phylogenetic relationships. Whole-genome sequencing was performed from lesion swabs of suspected cases. Phylogenetic analysis, incorporating clade Ia ( DQ011155.1 ) and global clade Ib sequences from GISAID, confirmed all Thai isolates as clade Ib. The genomes showed close relatedness to clade Ib viruses reported internationally, consistent with patients’ travel histories. All isolates exhibited a 1,140-bp deletion in the C3L gene region and 52–61 amino acid substitutions compared with the clade Ia reference. Genome sequences were submitted to GISAID and NCBI to support outbreak response and improve understanding of epidemiologic patterns, enabling more effective public health interventions. IMPORTANCE On 14 August 2024, the World Health Organization declared the monkeypox virus outbreak in Africa a public health emergency of international concern following the emergence of clade Ib. One week later, Thailand confirmed the first monkeypox clade Ib case in Asia. Through whole-genome sequencing and phylogenomic analysis of five cases detected between August 2024 and March 2025, we identified a 1,140-bp deletion in the C3L gene region that distinguishes clade Ib from clade Ia. This deletion directly affects the performance of some commercial real-time PCR assays designed for clade Ia, which rely on amplification of the missing genomic region. Our findings highlight important diagnostic limitations that could lead to misinterpretation or reduced sensitivity in routine testing. Beyond confirming regional introduction linked to global spread, this study underscores the critical role of genomic surveillance in ensuring accurate diagnosis, monitoring viral evolution, and strengthening outbreak response as monkeypox continues to expand internationally.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to understand the emergence of monkeypox virus clade Ib in Thailand and its implications for public health, as the World Health Organization declared a public health emergency of international concern due to sustained transmission and international spread following its emergence in the Democratic Republic of the Congo in August 2024.
Design and population
The study characterized the first five monkeypox virus clade Ib cases detected in Thailand between August 2024 and March 2025, assessing their phylogenetic relationships with global clade Ib sequences from GISAID and clade Ia reference sequences.
Core methods
Whole-genome sequencing was performed from lesion swabs of suspected cases, and phylogenomic analysis was conducted to compare the Thai isolates with clade Ia and global clade Ib sequences, identifying a 1,140-bp deletion in the C3L gene region and amino acid substitutions.
Main results
Phylogenetic analysis confirmed all Thai isolates as clade Ib, showing close relatedness to international clade Ib viruses and exhibiting a 1,140-bp deletion in the C3L gene region and 52–61 amino acid substitutions compared with the clade Ia reference.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings confirm the regional introduction of clade Ib monkeypox virus linked to global spread and highlight the importance of genomic surveillance in monitoring viral evolution and improving diagnostic accuracy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides critical insights for enhancing diagnostic testing, understanding transmission dynamics, and informing public health interventions as monkeypox continues to spread internationally.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to five cases and relies on genomic data, which may not fully capture the epidemiologic patterns, but the evidence is strong due to the use of global reference sequences and direct sequencing of Thai isolates.

猴痘病毒Clade Ib和Clade IIb在兔模型中的致病性比较研究

Comparative pathogenicity study of monkeypox virus Clade Ib and Clade IIb in a rabbit model
作者： Sreelekshmy Mohandas, Abhimanyu Kumar, Deepak Mali, Rajlaxmi Jain, Vishal Rajput, Deepak Y. Patil, Pragya Dhruv Yadav
摘要中文翻译摘要 猴痘 已成为一个重大的公共卫生问题,随着新变种的出现而蔓延到各大洲。来自最近全球 暴发 的进化枝 IIb 和新发现的进化枝 Ib 已显示出 传播 动态变化以及可能严重程度的变化。我们通过皮内和静脉注射途径的组合开发了 猴痘 新西兰白兔模型。感染后,兔子的皮肤上出现红斑丘疹性病变,到第二周时会发展为结痂和结痂,最终导致愈合和血清转化。Ib 和 IIb.A.2.1 谱系的感染表明,两种猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 变种都会引起皮肤和睾丸病变,以及鼻腔、皮肤和睾丸组织的病毒排泄。然而,两种变体之间的疾病严重程度没有显着差异。这些发现表明,该兔子模型可用于评估针对 MPXV 的对策。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 已成为一个重大的公共卫生问题,随着新变种的出现而蔓延到各大洲。该研究旨在比较猴痘病毒 Clade Ib 和 Clade IIb 的致病性。
设计与对象
该研究使用兔子模型进行,其中新西兰白兔感染了猴痘病毒 Clade Ib 和 Clade IIb.A.2.1 谱系。通过皮内和静脉途径进行感染。
核心方法
通过皮内和静脉注射途径的组合,为 猴痘 开发了新西兰白兔模型。该研究涉及观察兔子的病变进展和病毒排泄。
主要结果
Ib 和 IIb.A.2.1 谱系的感染表明,两种猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 变种都会引起皮肤和睾丸病变,以及鼻腔、皮肤和睾丸组织的病毒排泄。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,该兔子模型可用于评估针对 MPXV 的对策。该研究提供了在受控动物模型中了解猴痘病毒不同分支的致病性的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对 MPXV 的对策的制定提供信息,并有助于了解猴痘病毒不同分支的 传播 动态和严重性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于动物模型,并未考虑人类变异性。该证据是中等的,因为它是基于对照兔子模型,不包括人类临床数据。

Comparative pathogenicity study of monkeypox virus Clade Ib and Clade IIb in a rabbit model

Authors: Sreelekshmy Mohandas, Abhimanyu Kumar, Deepak Mali, Rajlaxmi Jain, Vishal Rajput, Deepak Y. Patil, Pragya Dhruv Yadav
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Mpox has emerged as a significant public health concern, spreading across continents with the emergence of new variants. Clade IIb, from the recent global outbreak, and the newly identified Clade Ib have shown changes in transmission dynamics and possibly in severity. We developed a New Zealand White rabbit model of Mpox by using a combination of intradermal and intravenous routes of administration. Following infection, the rabbits developed erythematous papular lesions on the skin, which progressed to crust and scab formation by the second week, ultimately leading to healing and seroconversion. Infection with the Clade Ib and IIb.A.2.1 lineages revealed that both Monkeypox virus (MPXV) variants caused skin and testicular lesions, as well as viral excretion from the nasal cavity, skin, and testicular tissue. However, there were no significant differences in disease severity between the two variants. These findings suggest that this rabbit model could be useful for evaluating countermeasures against MPXV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Mpox has emerged as a significant public health concern, spreading across continents with the emergence of new variants. The study aimed to compare the pathogenicity of Clade Ib and Clade IIb of the monkeypox virus.
Design and population
The study was conducted using a rabbit model, where New Zealand White rabbits were infected with the Monkeypox virus Clade Ib and Clade IIb.A.2.1 lineages. The infection was administered through intradermal and intravenous routes.
Core methods
A New Zealand White rabbit model was developed for Mpox by using a combination of intradermal and intravenous routes of administration. The study involved observing the progression of lesions and viral excretion in the rabbits.
Main results
Infection with the Clade Ib and IIb.A.2.1 lineages revealed that both Monkeypox virus (MPXV) variants caused skin and testicular lesions, as well as viral excretion from the nasal cavity, skin, and testicular tissue.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that this rabbit model could be useful for evaluating countermeasures against MPXV. The study provides insights into the pathogenicity of different clades of the monkeypox virus in a controlled animal model.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of countermeasures against MPXV and contribute to understanding the transmission dynamics and severity of different clades of the monkeypox virus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an animal model and does not account for human variability. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a controlled rabbit model and does not include human clinical data.

北卡罗来纳州,美国,2023年10月–2025年5月污水中发现的猴痘病毒监测

Monkeypox Virus Surveillance in Wastewater, North Carolina, USA, October 2023–May 2025
作者： Nicole L. Snyder, Thomas Clerkin, Sarah H. Koenigsberg, Mara B. Tate, Ty L. Lautenschlager, Justin Albertson, Emma L. Doran, Erica F. Wilson, Rachel T. Noble, Virginia T. Guidry
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决 猴痘病毒 潜在未被识别的 传播 的科学和公共卫生问题,因为北卡罗来纳州卫生与公众服务部在上次报告 5 个月后发现了 猴痘 分支 II 的新病例。
设计与对象
A wastewater surveillance study was conducted from October 29, 2023, to May 2025, across 16 treatment plants serving approximately 2 million residents. A total of 801 samples were collected to assess possible unrecognized transmission of mpox virus.
核心方法
使用 +17 天的窗口对废水检测和临床病例进行分析,以识别时间上一致的 猴痘 病例。该研究包括在选定的废水处理场进行非天花 正痘病毒 测试。
主要结果
在同一个排水区域中识别出>1个时间对齐的猴痘病例的总体灵敏度为10.6%(95% CI 6.5%-16.7%);特异性为95.1%(95% CI 93.2%-96.5%),阳性预测值为31.9%(95% CI 20.4%-46.2%),阴性预测值为83.2%(95% CI 80.3%-85.7%)。
解释与创新
研究发现,废水 监测 的敏感性较低,但为公共卫生应对提供了补充意识,凸显了其作为早期检测和监测工具的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,废水 监测 可能是识别 猴痘病毒 活动和支持公共卫生应对工作的有价值的方法,特别是在临床报告有限的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究检测 猴痘病毒 的敏感性较低,这可能限制了其对 监测 的有效性。然而,由于使用了定义的时间窗口以及对废水和临床数据的分析,证据的强度中等。

Monkeypox Virus Surveillance in Wastewater, North Carolina, USA, October 2023–May 2025

Authors: Nicole L. Snyder, Thomas Clerkin, Sarah H. Koenigsberg, Mara B. Tate, Ty L. Lautenschlager, Justin Albertson, Emma L. Doran, Erica F. Wilson, Rachel T. Noble, Virginia T. Guidry
Original abstract (English)
In September 2023, the North Carolina Department of Health and Human Services identified a new case of mpox clade II 5 months after the last previous report. To assess possible unrecognized transmission, nonvariola orthopoxvirus testing was implemented at select wastewater sites. During October 29, 2023-May 2025, a total of 801 samples were collected across 16 treatment plants serving ≈2 million residents. We analyzed wastewater detections (n = 47) and clinical cases (n = 45) using a +17-day window. Overall sensitivity of identifying the presence of >1 temporally aligned mpox case within a sewershed was 10.6% (95% CI 6.5%-16.7%); specificity was 95.1% (95% CI 93.2%-96.5%), positive predictive value was 31.9% (95% CI 20.4%-46.2%), and negative predictive value was 83.2% (95% CI 80.3%-85.7%). Restricting detections above the limit of detection reduced sensitivity but increased specificity. Overall, wastewater surveillance showed low sensitivity but provided complementary awareness for public health response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the scientific and public health problem of potential unrecognized transmission of mpox virus, as the North Carolina Department of Health and Human Services identified a new case of mpox clade II 5 months after the last previous report.
Design and population
A wastewater surveillance study was conducted from October 29, 2023, to May 2025, across 16 treatment plants serving approximately 2 million residents. A total of 801 samples were collected to assess possible unrecognized transmission of mpox virus.
Core methods
Wastewater detections and clinical cases were analyzed using a +17-day window to identify temporally aligned mpox cases. The study included nonvariola orthopoxvirus testing at select wastewater sites.
Main results
Overall sensitivity of identifying the presence of >1 temporally aligned mpox case within a sewershed was 10.6% (95% CI 6.5%-16.7%); specificity was 95.1% (95% CI 93.2%-96.5%), positive predictive value was 31.9% (95% CI 20.4%-46.2%), and negative predictive value was 83.2% (95% CI 80.3%-85.7%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that wastewater surveillance showed low sensitivity but provided complementary awareness for public health response, highlighting its potential as a tool for early detection and monitoring.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that wastewater surveillance could be a valuable method for identifying mpox virus activity and supporting public health response efforts, particularly in areas with limited clinical reporting.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study had low sensitivity in detecting mpox virus, which may limit its effectiveness for surveillance. However, the evidence is moderate in strength due to the use of a defined temporal window and analysis of both wastewater and clinical data.

在DRC数据支持下分析猴痘干预措施在存在环境传播途径中的情况

Analysis of Mpox intervention measures in presence of environmental transmission pathway using data from DRC
作者： T. L. Gasebonno, J. B. H. Njagarah, S.O. Akindeinde, E.M. Lungu, T.L. Gasebonno, J.B.H. Njagarah, Saheed Ojo Akindeinde
摘要中文翻译本文开发了一个 猴痘 传播 动态数学模型,用于解释通过人际接触以及微生物感染环境产生的疾病 传播。对两个病例的模型的地方性平衡进行了评估。(1) 假设环境中的微生物对疾病的传播没有作用,并且证明地方病平衡是唯一的,当R 0 大于1时存在,并且全局渐近稳定。(2) 对于环境微生物浓度在疾病传播中起作用的情况,模型被证明可能在R 0 处表现出向后分岔。这表明,当疾病已经流行时,由于稳定​​的流行平衡,将 R 0 降低到 1 以下可能无法消除 猴痘。基于 DRC 的 暴发 数据的数值结果表明: (1)人际接触增加有可能使疾病恶化,(2)高隔离率和感染个体的加速康复通过任何可能的干预可以轻松遏制疾病。结果进一步表明,环境污染并没有显着影响疾病 传播 的发病率。更重要的是,据观察,实施有影响力的缓解措施会导致感染人数的峰值降低和延迟。这对于医疗保健系统在最需要时为更多紧急情况和隔离设施做好准备至关重要,特别是在资源匮乏的社区或国家。我们的研究结果强调需要针对行为和环境因素采取综合干预措施,特别是在资源有限的环境中。

设计与对象
对两个病例的模型的地方性平衡进行了评估。

(2) 对于环境微生物浓度在疾病传播中起作用的情况,模型被证明可能在R 0 处表现出向后分岔。

Analysis of Mpox intervention measures in presence of environmental transmission pathway using data from DRC

Authors: T. L. Gasebonno, J. B. H. Njagarah, S.O. Akindeinde, E.M. Lungu, T.L. Gasebonno, J.B.H. Njagarah, Saheed Ojo Akindeinde
Original abstract (English)
This paper develops a mathematical model for Mpox transmission dynamics accounting for disease transmission through interpersonal contact as well as from microbes infested environment. The endemic equilibrium of the model was evaluated for two cases. (1) When it is assumed that the microbes in the environment play no role in the spread of the disease, and it was shown that the endemic equilibrium is unique, exists when R 0 is greater than unity, and is globally asymptotically stable. (2) For the case when environmental microbes concentration plays a role in disease transmission, the model was shown to possibly exhibit backward bifurcation at R 0 . This indicates that lowering R 0 below 1 may not eliminate Mpox when the disease is already endemic, due to the stable endemic equilibrium. Numerical results, based on outbreak data from the DRC indicate that; (1) increased interpersonal contact has the potential of making the disease worse, (2) high isolation rate and accelerated recovery of infected individuals through any possible intervention can easily contain the disease. The results further show that environmental contamination does not substantially influence disease transmission rates. More still, it is observed that implementation of influential mitigation measures results in lower and delayed peaks of the number of infected. This is essential for healthcare systems to prepare for more emergency cases and isolation facilities when needed most especially in under resourced communities or countries. Our findings highlight the need for comprehensive interventions addressing both behavioural and environmental factors, especially in environments with limited resources.

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

基于高内涵图像的筛选揭示痘苗病毒膜蛋白是 LC3 脂化的潜在调节剂。

High-content image-based screening reveals vaccinia virus membrane proteins as potential regulators of LC3 lipidation.
作者： Melanie Krause, Artur Yakimovich, Noemi Vágó, Ingo Drexler, Jason Mercer
摘要中文翻译未标记:自噬是一种用于降解细胞器和蛋白质的分解代谢过程,并且在感染过程中它还充当一种细胞防御机制,称为异体自噬。痘苗病毒 (VACV) 是一种原型痘病毒,仅在 宿主 细胞的细胞质中复制。据了解,VACV 感染会导致 LC3 脂化,同时阻止自噬体形成,但尚未确定负责的病毒蛋白。使用感染时程蛋白质印迹和磷脂酶 D 带移测定,我们表明 VACV 驱动真正的 LC3-II 形成,而不是阻碍前 LC3 裂解,并且这取决于早期病毒基因表达。为了鉴定所涉及的基因,我们生成了 dsRed-LC3-I-GFP 报告细胞系,并开发了一种基于单细胞 LC3 粒度的基于图像的筛选方法,并将其应用于 80 个早期 VACV 基因。筛选显示,VACV 诱导的脂质化是双向调节的。后续验证确定 H3 为负调节因子,膜蛋白 A14、L5 和 G5 为 LC3 脂化的正调节因子。这些发现提供了影响 LC3 脂化的 VACV 基因的第一个系统图谱,并表明 VACV 和自噬之间的相互作用比以前认为的更有针对性。重要性:痘病毒,包括牛痘病毒和密切相关的猴痘病毒,仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题,并且越来越多地被用作 疫苗 和癌症免疫疗法 媒介。因此,了解这些病毒如何操纵 宿主 防御对于 抗病毒药物 和生物技术应用都很重要。自噬是一种关键的细胞防御途径,已知痘苗病毒会触发自噬蛋白 LC3 的脂化,同时阻止有效的自噬,尽管驱动这一过程的病毒基因仍然未知。使用基于图像的筛选策略,我们鉴定了几个增加或减少 LC3 脂化的早期痘苗病毒基因,表明该病毒在两个方向上积极调节这一过程。这些发现揭示了痘苗病毒与 宿主 自噬之间的相互作用比之前认识的更有针对性,并为研究病毒-自噬相互作用提供了一种广泛适用的筛选方法。

High-content image-based screening reveals vaccinia virus membrane proteins as potential regulators of LC3 lipidation.

Authors: Melanie Krause, Artur Yakimovich, Noemi Vágó, Ingo Drexler, Jason Mercer
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: Autophagy is a catabolic process used for the degradation of organelles and proteins, and it also functions as a cellular defense mechanism, known as xenophagy, during infection. Vaccinia virus (VACV), the prototypic poxvirus, replicates exclusively in the cytoplasm of host cells. It is known that VACV infection causes LC3 lipidation while preventing autophagosome formation, yet the viral protein(s) responsible have not been identified. Using infection time-course Western blotting and a phospholipase D band-shift assay, we show that VACV drives genuine LC3-II formation, rather than a block in pro-LC3 cleavage, and that this depends on early viral gene expression. To identify the gene(s) involved, we generated a dsRed-LC3-I-GFP reporter cell line and developed an image-based screening approach based on single-cell LC3 granularity, which we applied to 80 early VACV genes. The screen revealed that VACV-induced lipidation is regulated in both directions. Follow-up validation identified H3 as a negative regulator and the membrane proteins A14, L5, and G5 as positive contributors to LC3 lipidation. These findings provide the first systematic map of VACV genes affecting LC3 lipidation and suggest that the interplay between VACV and autophagy is more directed than previously thought. IMPORTANCE: Poxviruses, including vaccinia virus and the closely related monkeypox virus, remain a significant public health concern and are increasingly exploited as vaccine and cancer immunotherapy vectors. Understanding how these viruses manipulate host defenses is therefore important for both antiviral and biotechnology applications. Autophagy is a key cellular defense pathway, and vaccinia virus is known to trigger lipidation of the autophagy protein LC3 while blocking productive autophagy, although the viral genes driving this remained unknown. Using an image-based screening strategy, we identify several early vaccinia virus genes that increase or decrease LC3 lipidation, showing that the virus actively regulates this process in both directions. These findings reveal a more targeted interaction between vaccinia virus and host autophagy than previously recognized and provide a broadly applicable screening approach for studying virus-autophagy interactions.

It is known that VACV infection causes LC3 lipidation while preventing autophagosome formation, yet the viral protein(s) responsible have not been identified.

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

西班牙 猴痘 确诊病例的城市规模和流行病学特征

Municipality size and epidemiological characteristics among confirmed mpox cases in Spain
作者： Aina March-Yagüe, Carles Pericas, Àngela Domínguez, Diana Toledo, Cristina Rius, Miquel Amengual-Moreno, Isabel Marcos, Lluïsa Forns, Lídia Arranz-Martín, Lluís Forcadell-Díez
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2022 年,多州 猴痘 暴发 代表了新的全球健康挑战,并强调性接触是 传播 的主要途径。西班牙报告的病例数是欧洲最多的,但其境内病例分布不规则。
设计与对象
这是一项针对 2022 年 6 月至 2023 年 1 月期间在西班牙七个地区确诊的 16 岁以上男性 猴痘 病例的横断面研究。该研究旨在描述居住在大城市与小城市的个体中 猴痘 病例的特征,并研究城市规模是否与 猴痘 发病率 以及确诊病例的流行病学和临床特征相关。
核心方法
对大城市(人口≥ 200,000)和较小城市的流行病学和临床变量进行了分析。通过逻辑回归模型获得具有 95% 置信区间 (CI95%) 的比值比 (aOR),以描述个体中 猴痘 病例的特征。为了根据城市规模评估累积 发病率 的差异,通过具有稳健方差的泊松回归模型获得累积 发病率 比率 (aCIR CI95%)。
主要结果
Among the 1,162 reported mpox cases, 776 (66.8%) were from large urban areas. Living in a large city was significantly associated with being born in low-income countries (aOR = 2.1, 1.2–3.5), having a previous sexually transmitted infection (STI) (aOR = 1.6, 1.1–2.3), participating in chemsex (aOR = 1.7, 1.0–2.7), having sex in private leisure venues (aOR = 1.5, 1.1–2.2), using pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP) (aOR = 1.7, 1.1–2.5), and regularly using condoms during casual sex (aOR = 1.9, 1.3–2.9). Clinical complications were less frequent in large cities (aOR = 0.4, 0.2–0.7). Living in a large city was associated with a higher cumulative incidence of mpox (aCIR = 2.7; 1.9–3.7) compared with living in smaller municipalities.
解释与创新
这些发现支持在规划 猴痘 的公共卫生应对措施时考虑城市规模和城市社会动态。
科研与公卫意义
进行了描述性分析。它还与化学性行为、私人休闲场所的性接触以及之前的性传播感染 诊断 相关的特征有关。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,未建立因果关系。样本量足以进行描述性分析,但可能不足以用于更复杂的统计模型。该研究并未控制所有潜在的混杂因素,研究结果的普遍性可能仅限于所研究的地区和人群。

Municipality size and epidemiological characteristics among confirmed mpox cases in Spain

Authors: Aina March-Yagüe, Carles Pericas, Àngela Domínguez, Diana Toledo, Cristina Rius, Miquel Amengual-Moreno, Isabel Marcos, Lluïsa Forns, Lídia Arranz-Martín, Lluís Forcadell-Díez
Original abstract (English)
In 2022, a multistate mpox outbreak represented a new global health challenge and highlighted sexual contact as the main route of transmission. Spain reported the highest number of cases in Europe but showed an irregular distribution of cases across its territory. This study aims to describe the characteristics of mpox cases among individuals living in large cities versus smaller municipalities, and to examine whether municipality size is associated with mpox incidence and with epidemiological and clinical characteristics among confirmed cases. This is a cross‐sectional study of mpox cases in men aged ≥ 16 years confirmed between June 2022 and January 2023 in seven Spanish regions. Epidemiological and clinical variables were analysed in large cities (population ≥ 200 000) and smaller municipalities. Descriptive analyses were performed. Odds ratios (aOR) with 95% confidence intervals (CI95%) were obtained through logistic regression models to describe characteristics of mpox cases among individuals. To assess differences in cumulative incidence according to municipality size, cumulative incidence ratios (aCIR CI95%) were obtained through Poisson regression models with robust variance. Among the 1,162 reported mpox cases, 776 (66.8%) were from large urban areas. Among mpox cases, living in large cities was significantly associated with being born in low-income countries (aOR = 2.1, 1.2–3.5), having a previous sexually transmitted infection (STI) (aOR = 1.6, 1.1–2.3), participating in chemsex (aOR = 1.7, 1.0–2.7), having sex in private leisure venues (aOR = 1.5, 1.1–2.2), using pre-exposure prophylaxis (PrEP) (aOR = 1.7, 1.1–2.5), and regularly using condoms during casual sex (aOR = 1.9, 1.3–2.9). Clinical complications were less frequent in large cities (aOR = 0.4, 0.2–0.7). Living in a large city was associated with a higher cumulative incidence of mpox (aCIR = 2.7; 1.9–3.7) compared with living in smaller municipalities. Living in a large city was associated with a higher cumulative incidence of mpox. It was also associated with characteristics linked to chemsex practices, sexual encounters in private leisure venues and previous STI diagnosis. These findings support considering municipality size and urban social dynamics when planning public health responses to mpox.
View research seven-element analysis

In 2022, a multistate mpox outbreak represented a new global health challenge and highlighted sexual contact as the main route of transmission. Spain reported the highest number of cases in Europe but showed an irregular distribution of cases across its territory.

This is a cross-sectional study of mpox cases in men aged ≥ 16 years confirmed between June 2022 and January 2023 in seven Spanish regions. Clinical complications were less frequent in large cities (aOR = 0.4, 0.2–0.7). Living in a large city was associated with a higher cumulative incidence of mpox (aCIR = 2.7; 1.9–3.7) compared with living in smaller municipalities.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings support considering municipality size and urban social dynamics when planning public health responses to mpox.
Scientific and public-health significance
Descriptive analyses were performed. It was also associated with characteristics linked to chemsex practices, sexual encounters in private leisure venues and previous STI diagnosis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which does not establish causality. The sample size is adequate for descriptive analysis but may not be sufficient for more complex statistical models. The study does not control for all potential confounders, and the generalizability of the findings may be limited to the regions and population studied.

特考韦瑞 抗性 猴痘病毒 的体外选择和表征。

In Vitro Selection and Characterization of Tecovirimat-resistant Mpox Virus.
作者： Kei Konishi, Haruka Maeda, Misato Shibazaki, Takao Shishido, Manabu Igarashi, William W Hall, Michihito Sasaki, Yasuko Orba, Hirofumi Sawa, Akihiko Sato
摘要中文翻译BACKGROUND: 猴痘病毒 (MPXV) is the causative agent of 猴痘,a disease characterized by skin lesions,and remains a global threat since the recent 2022 暴发. 特考韦瑞 is an 抗病毒药物 agent that inhibits MPXV replication in vitro and in vivo by targeting the viral phospholipase VP37 protein and blocking viral envelope wrapping. Although 特考韦瑞 has been administered to many 猴痘 patients,the emergence of 特考韦瑞-resistant MPXVs carrying 突变 in the VP37 protein has been reported,particularly in immunocompromised individuals.然而,这些 突变 对药物敏感性和病毒适应性的影响尚未得到充分表征。方法:在体外应用 特考韦瑞 作为选择压力来分离携带临床观察到的 VP37 取代的 MPXV 变体。比较了主要 VP37 突变 的药物敏感性,并评估了分离病毒的细胞适应性。RESULTS: The VP37-N267D and -Y258C mutants showed markedly reduced susceptibility to 特考韦瑞 compared with the VP37-I372N and -T245I (+I7L-A388S) mutants,while retaining susceptibility to an alternative anti-MPXV agent,brincidofovir. The VP37-I372N,T245I (+I7L-A388S),Y258C,and N267D mutants exhibited viral fitness comparable to that of the parental virus in A549 and Vero cells,as well as in MRC-5 and HaCaT cells. CONCLUSIONS: These findings provide insights into the phenotype of each VP37 突变 and emphasize the importance of developing 抗病毒药物 with distinct mechanisms of action to effectively combat 特考韦瑞-resistant MPXV.
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 猴痘病毒 VP37 蛋白中特定 突变 对药物敏感性和病毒适应性的影响,弥补有关 特考韦瑞 耐药变体的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究涉及携带临床观察到的VP37取代的特考韦瑞抗性猴痘病毒变体的体外选择和表征,重点关注不同细胞系中的一系列VP37 突变。
核心方法
特考韦瑞 用作选择性压力来分离具有 VP37 取代的 MPXV 变体。在 A549、Vero、MRC-5 和 HaCaT 细胞中评估药物敏感性和病毒适应性。
主要结果
与 VP37-I372N 和 -T245I (+I7L-A388S) 突变体相比,VP37-N267D 和 -Y258C 突变体表现出对 特考韦瑞 的敏感性降低,同时保留对布西多福韦的敏感性。所有测试的突变体在各种细胞系中都表现出与亲本病毒相当的适应性。
解释与创新
这些发现提供了对每个 VP37 特考韦瑞 表型的深入了解,强调了开发具有独特作用机制的 特考韦瑞 来对抗 特考韦瑞 抗性 MPXV 的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为新的 抗病毒药物 策略的开发提供信息,并提高对 MPXV 耐药机制的理解,有助于未来 暴发 的管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用体外模型和需要进一步体内验证。由于该研究的体外性质,证据强度中等。

In Vitro Selection and Characterization of Tecovirimat-resistant Mpox Virus.

Authors: Kei Konishi, Haruka Maeda, Misato Shibazaki, Takao Shishido, Manabu Igarashi, William W Hall, Michihito Sasaki, Yasuko Orba, Hirofumi Sawa, Akihiko Sato
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Mpox virus (MPXV) is the causative agent of mpox, a disease characterized by skin lesions, and remains a global threat since the recent 2022 outbreak. Tecovirimat is an antiviral agent that inhibits MPXV replication in vitro and in vivo by targeting the viral phospholipase VP37 protein and blocking viral envelope wrapping. Although tecovirimat has been administered to many mpox patients, the emergence of tecovirimat-resistant MPXVs carrying mutations in the VP37 protein has been reported, particularly in immunocompromised individuals. However, the impact of these respective mutations on drug susceptibility and viral fitness have not been fully characterized. METHODS: Tecovirimat was applied as a selective pressure in vitro to isolate MPXV variants carrying clinically observed VP37 substitutions. Drug susceptibility among the major VP37 mutations was compared, and the cellular fitness of the isolated viruses was evaluated. RESULTS: The VP37-N267D and -Y258C mutants showed markedly reduced susceptibility to tecovirimat compared with the VP37-I372N and -T245I (+I7L-A388S) mutants, while retaining susceptibility to an alternative anti-MPXV agent, brincidofovir. The VP37-I372N, T245I (+I7L-A388S), Y258C, and N267D mutants exhibited viral fitness comparable to that of the parental virus in A549 and Vero cells, as well as in MRC-5 and HaCaT cells. CONCLUSIONS: These findings provide insights into the phenotype of each VP37 mutation and emphasize the importance of developing antivirals with distinct mechanisms of action to effectively combat tecovirimat-resistant MPXV.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the impact of specific mutations in the VP37 protein of mpox virus on drug susceptibility and viral fitness, addressing the knowledge gap regarding tecovirimat-resistant variants.
Design and population
The study involved in vitro selection and characterization of tecovirimat-resistant mpox virus variants carrying clinically observed VP37 substitutions, focusing on a range of VP37 mutations in different cell lines.
Core methods
Tecovirimat was used as a selective pressure to isolate MPXV variants with VP37 substitutions. Drug susceptibility and viral fitness were evaluated in A549, Vero, MRC-5, and HaCaT cells.
Main results
The VP37-N267D and -Y258C mutants showed reduced susceptibility to tecovirimat compared to the VP37-I372N and -T245I (+I7L-A388S) mutants, while retaining susceptibility to brincidofovir. All tested mutants exhibited comparable fitness to the parental virus in various cell lines.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide insights into the phenotype of each VP37 mutation, highlighting the importance of developing antivirals with distinct mechanisms of action to combat tecovirimat-resistant MPXV.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform the development of new antiviral strategies and improve understanding of MPXV resistance mechanisms, aiding in the management of future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of in vitro models and the need for further validation in vivo. The evidence strength is moderate due to the in vitro nature of the study.

人猴痘病毒分支 1b 与水痘带状疱疹病毒的共循环,乌干达

Cocirculation of Human Monkeypox Virus Clade 1b with Varicella Zoster Virus, Uganda
作者： Nicholas Bbosa, Alfred Ssekagiri, Hamidah S. Namagembe, Stella E. Nabirye, Ronald Kiiza, Danstan Kabuuka, Stephen Balinandi, Tom Lutalo, John Kayiwa, Henry Kyobe Bosa
摘要中文翻译2024 年 7 月,我们报告了乌干达首例人类 猴痘 病例的信息。临床疑似病例(2024 年 7 月至 12 月)的基因组特征显示,多个猴痘病毒 1b 分支簇和水痘带状疱疹病毒存在共循环。我们的研究结果强调需要通过分子技术完善鉴别诊断并加强 监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查乌干达人猴痘病毒 1b 分支与水痘带状疱疹病毒的共循环,解决了解潜在诊断挑战和共感染动态的需要。
设计与对象
该研究分析了2024年7月至12月乌干达临床疑似猴痘病例,收集了1072份样本。确诊病例主要集中在16-30岁之间,性别分布几乎相等。
核心方法
使用实时 PCR 检测样本中是否存在猴痘病毒,并对一部分阴性样本进行测序以识别其他潜在病原体。对流行的 MPXV 和其他病毒进行了系统发育表征和突变分析。
主要结果
基因组表征揭示了几种猴痘病毒 1b 分支簇和水痘带状疱疹病毒的共循环。在 1,072 份样本中,193 份 MPXV 检测呈阳性,879 份检测呈阴性,其中一部分样本经过测序以确定其他病原体。
解释与创新
研究结果证明猴痘病毒 1b 分支和水痘带状疱疹病毒在乌干达共存,这表明分子技术对于准确区分 诊断 的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了改进 猴痘 和诊断方法的必要性,以将 猴痘 与其他引起皮疹的感染区分开来,特别是在 猴痘 患病率 高的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖 PCR 检测和对阴性样本子集的分析而受到限制。由于该研究的观察性质和误诊的可能性,证据是中等的。

Cocirculation of Human Monkeypox Virus Clade 1b with Varicella Zoster Virus, Uganda

Authors: Nicholas Bbosa, Alfred Ssekagiri, Hamidah S. Namagembe, Stella E. Nabirye, Ronald Kiiza, Danstan Kabuuka, Stephen Balinandi, Tom Lutalo, John Kayiwa, Henry Kyobe Bosa
Original abstract (English)
In July 2024, we reported information on the initial human mpox cases in Uganda. Genomic characterization of clinically suspected cases (July–December 2024) revealed cocirculation of several monkeypox virus clade 1b clusters and varicella zoster virus. Our findings highlight the need to refine differential diagnoses by molecular techniques and strengthen surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the cocirculation of human monkeypox virus clade 1b with varicella zoster virus in Uganda, addressing the need to understand potential diagnostic challenges and co-infection dynamics.
Design and population
The study analyzed clinically suspected mpox cases in Uganda from July to December 2024, with 1,072 samples collected. Confirmed cases were predominantly in individuals aged 16–30 years, with a nearly equal gender distribution.
Core methods
Real-time PCR was used to test samples for monkeypox virus, and a subset of negative samples was sequenced to identify other potential pathogens. Phylogenetic characterization and mutational analysis were performed on the circulating MPXV and other viruses.
Main results
Genomic characterization revealed cocirculation of several monkeypox virus clade 1b clusters and varicella zoster virus. Of the 1,072 samples, 193 tested positive for MPXV, while 879 tested negative, with a subset sequenced to identify other pathogens.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate the coexistence of monkeypox virus clade 1b and varicella zoster virus in Uganda, suggesting the importance of molecular techniques for accurate differential diagnosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study underscores the necessity for improved surveillance and diagnostic methods to distinguish mpox from other rash-causing infections, particularly in regions with high mpox prevalence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on PCR testing and the analysis of a subset of negative samples. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and potential for misdiagnosis.

猴痘 传播 在 疫苗接种、隔离和行为响应下的 SEQIRV 模型的动力学和分叉分析

Dynamics and bifurcation analysis of an SEQIRV model for Mpox transmission under vaccination, quarantine, and behavioural responses
作者： Amit Kumer Mondal, Md Hamidul Islam
摘要中文翻译2022年猴痘病毒的重新出现表明需要有效的干预策略来预防疾病。本研究开发了一个确定性SEQIRV模型来了解疾病动态,并分析了适应性疫苗接种策略、计划外检疫措施以及行为反应对疾病传播的影响。该模型还通过将区室流行病学模型与动态 传播 分析相结合,研究了靶向 疫苗接种 在几个人群中的影响,例如高危人群、男男性行为者 (MSM) 和医护人员。我们研究了不同的 疫苗接种 策略如何影响分叉方向,并分析了各种初始暴露和感染人群水平的相应吸引盆地。结果显示,最初的高 疫苗接种 率不如在整个 暴发 期间维持高 疫苗接种 率有效,这表明持续的 疫苗接种 努力对于长期的疫情控制至关重要。此外,尽早实施隔离,大大降低了疫情高峰;另一方面,检疫泄漏削弱了检疫的有效性,并可能加剧疾病的持续存在。结果还表明,行为反应,例如提高认识和政府诱导的限制,可以显着减少疾病传播。最后,本研究确定了关键干预策略,以支持决策者设计和实施针对 猴痘 等新出现的人畜共患疾病的有效控制措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2022年猴痘病毒的重新出现表明需要有效的干预策略来预防疾病。
设计与对象
本研究开发了一个确定性SEQIRV模型来了解疾病动态,并分析了适应性疫苗接种策略、计划外检疫措施以及行为反应对疾病传播的影响。
核心方法
该模型还通过将区室流行病学模型与动态 传播 分析相结合,研究了靶向 疫苗接种 在几个人群中的影响,例如高危人群、男男性行为者 (MSM) 和医护人员。
主要结果
我们研究了不同的 疫苗接种 策略如何影响分叉方向,并分析了各种初始暴露和感染人群水平的相应吸引盆地。
解释与创新
结果显示,最初的高 疫苗接种 率不如在整个 暴发 期间维持高 疫苗接种 率有效,这表明持续的 疫苗接种 努力对于长期的疫情控制至关重要。
科研与公卫意义
此外,尽早实施隔离,大大降低了疫情高峰;另一方面,检疫泄漏削弱了检疫的有效性,并可能加剧疾病的持续存在。
局限与证据强度
结果还表明,行为反应,例如提高认识和政府诱导的限制,可以显着减少疾病传播。

Dynamics and bifurcation analysis of an SEQIRV model for Mpox transmission under vaccination, quarantine, and behavioural responses

Authors: Amit Kumer Mondal, Md Hamidul Islam
Original abstract (English)
The re-emergence of the Mpox virus in 2022 indicates the need for effective intervention strategies to prevent disease. This study develops a deterministic SEQIRV model to understand the disease dynamics and also analyzes the adaptive vaccination strategies, unscheduled quarantine measures, and effect of behavioral responses on disease transmission. The model also investigates the effects of targeted vaccination in several population groups, for example, high-risk groups, men who have sex with men (MSM), and healthcare workers, by combining compartmental epidemiological modeling with dynamic transmission analysis. We investigate how different vaccination strategies influence the direction of bifurcation and analyze the corresponding basins of attraction for various initial exposed and infected population levels. The results reveal that an initially high vaccination rate is less effective than maintaining a high vaccination rate throughout the outbreak, suggesting that sustained vaccination efforts are essential for long-term epidemic control. In addition, early implementation of quarantine significantly reduces the epidemic peak; on the other hand, quarantine leakage weakens the effectiveness of quarantine and may fuel disease persistence. The results also demonstrate that behavioral responses, such as increased awareness and government-induced restrictions, significantly reduce disease spread. Finally, this study identifies key intervention strategies to support policymakers in designing and implementing effective control measures against emerging zoonotic diseases such as Mpox.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The re-emergence of the Mpox virus in 2022 indicates the need for effective intervention strategies to prevent disease.
Design and population
This study develops a deterministic SEQIRV model to understand the disease dynamics and also analyzes the adaptive vaccination strategies, unscheduled quarantine measures, and effect of behavioral responses on disease transmission.
Core methods
The model also investigates the effects of targeted vaccination in several population groups, for example, high-risk groups, men who have sex with men (MSM), and healthcare workers, by combining compartmental epidemiological modeling with dynamic transmission analysis.
Main results
We investigate how different vaccination strategies influence the direction of bifurcation and analyze the corresponding basins of attraction for various initial exposed and infected population levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The results reveal that an initially high vaccination rate is less effective than maintaining a high vaccination rate throughout the outbreak, suggesting that sustained vaccination efforts are essential for long-term epidemic control.
Scientific and public-health significance
In addition, early implementation of quarantine significantly reduces the epidemic peak; on the other hand, quarantine leakage weakens the effectiveness of quarantine and may fuel disease persistence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The results also demonstrate that behavioral responses, such as increased awareness and government-induced restrictions, significantly reduce disease spread.

塞拉利昂医疗机构中的猴痘病毒,2025 年 6 月

Monkeypox Virus in Healthcare Settings, Sierra Leone, June 2025
作者： Michaella Jaba, Emmanuel Saidu, Emmanuel S. Kamanda, Christina Frederick, Laurens Liesenborghs, Megan Halbrook, Nicole A. Hoff, Placide Mbala-Kingebeni, Isaac I. Bogoch, Souradet Y. Shaw
摘要中文翻译在 2025 年塞拉利昂 猴痘 暴发 期间,我们评估了 2 家医院的环境污染。在 89 个表面采样中,有 6 个(6.7%)表面样本(主要来自门)猴痘病毒 PCR 呈阳性。我们的研究结果强调了医疗机构中猴痘病毒的表面污染,并有助于在资源有限的环境中优先考虑感染预防和控制目标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在 2025 年塞拉利昂 猴痘 暴发 期间,该研究旨在评估医疗机构中的环境污染。
设计与对象
该研究于 2025 年 猴痘 暴发 期间在塞拉利昂的 2 家医院进行,对 89 个表面进行了采样。
核心方法
对环境表面进行采样并使用 PCR 进行测试,以检测猴痘病毒的存在。
主要结果
在 89 个表面采样中,有 6 个(6.7%)表面样本(主要来自门)猴痘病毒 PCR 呈阳性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在 暴发 期间,医疗机构的表面上存在猴痘病毒,这在这方面是一个新颖的观察结果。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了表面净化的必要性,为医疗保健环境中的感染预防和控制策略提供了信息,特别是在资源有限的环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于2家医院的环境采样,证据基于PCR结果,可能无法捕获病毒传播的所有方面。

Monkeypox Virus in Healthcare Settings, Sierra Leone, June 2025

Authors: Michaella Jaba, Emmanuel Saidu, Emmanuel S. Kamanda, Christina Frederick, Laurens Liesenborghs, Megan Halbrook, Nicole A. Hoff, Placide Mbala-Kingebeni, Isaac I. Bogoch, Souradet Y. Shaw
Original abstract (English)
During the 2025 mpox outbreak in Sierra Leone, we assessed environmental contamination in 2 hospitals. Of 89 surfaces sampled, 6 (6.7%) surface samples, primarily from doors, were PCR-positive for monkeypox virus. Our findings highlight monkeypox virus surface contamination in healthcare settings and help prioritize infection prevention and control targets in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
During the 2025 mpox outbreak in Sierra Leone, the study aimed to assess environmental contamination in healthcare settings.
Design and population
The study was conducted in 2 hospitals during the 2025 mpox outbreak in Sierra Leone, with 89 surfaces sampled.
Core methods
Environmental surfaces were sampled and tested using PCR to detect the presence of monkeypox virus.
Main results
Of 89 surfaces sampled, 6 (6.7%) surface samples, primarily from doors, were PCR-positive for monkeypox virus.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate the presence of monkeypox virus on surfaces in healthcare settings during an outbreak, which is a novel observation in this context.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform infection prevention and control strategies in healthcare settings, particularly in resource-limited environments, by highlighting the need for surface decontamination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to environmental sampling in 2 hospitals, and the evidence is based on PCR results, which may not capture all aspects of viral transmission.

猴痘 的临床模仿者:热带转诊中心的病例系列

Clinical Mimickers of Mpox: A Case Series from a Tropical Referral Center
作者： Silvia Hees de Carvalho, Adriana de Souza Andrade, Janaina de Moura Freire, Marco Antônio Campos, Erna Geessien Kroon
摘要中文翻译猴痘 是一种由猴痘病毒引起的人畜共患疾病,猴痘病毒是一种属于 正痘病毒 属的双链 DNA 病毒。世界卫生组织 (WHO) 于 2022 年 7 月宣布 猴痘 为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件 (PHEIC),其中全球分类 IIb 暴发 属于国际关注的突发公共卫生事件 (PHEIC),并于 2024 年 8 月在刚果民主共和国与 Ia 和 Ib 分类相关的病例增加后再次宣布该事件为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件 (PHEIC)。尽管人们的认识有所提高,但诊断 猴痘 仍然具有挑战性,因为其临床表现常常与其他感染性和非感染性疾病的临床表现重叠。这种诊断挑战在不同地理环境中都会发生,但在热带地区,由于当地流行的感染(包括登革热和基孔肯雅热等虫媒病毒性疾病)的传播,可能会变得更加复杂。在这里,我们描述了一系列最初评估为疑似 猴痘 的 9 名患者,随后对这些患者进行了替代诊断。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
猴痘 是一种由猴痘病毒引起的人畜共患疾病,猴痘病毒是一种属于 正痘病毒 属的双链 DNA 病毒。世界卫生组织 (WHO) 于 2022 年 7 月宣布 猴痘 为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件 (PHEIC),其中全球分类 IIb 暴发 属于国际关注的突发公共卫生事件 (PHEIC),并于 2024 年 8 月在刚果民主共和国与 Ia 和 Ib 分类相关的病例增加后再次宣布该事件为国际关注的突发公共卫生事件 (PHEIC)。
设计与对象
这种诊断挑战在不同地理环境中都会发生,但在热带地区,由于当地流行的感染(包括登革热和基孔肯雅热等虫媒病毒性疾病)的传播,可能会变得更加复杂。在这里,我们描述了一系列最初评估为疑似 猴痘 的 9 名患者,随后对这些患者进行了替代诊断。
核心方法
该研究涉及九名患者的病例系列,最初怀疑患有 猴痘,并通过临床评估和实验室测试证实了其他诊断。
主要结果
尽管人们的认识有所提高,但诊断 猴痘 仍然具有挑战性,因为其临床表现常常与其他感染性和非感染性疾病的临床表现重叠。
解释与创新
作者强调了 猴痘 由于其临床特征与其他病症重叠而导致的诊断困难,并强调在热带环境中需要准确区分 诊断。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了在 猴痘 病例中考虑替代诊断的重要性,特别是在其他感染流行的地区,以提高诊断准确性和患者管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于案例系列,可能无法涵盖 猴痘 演示的全部内容。证据是温和的,因为它是基于临床观察而不是对照实验。

Clinical Mimickers of Mpox: A Case Series from a Tropical Referral Center

Authors: Silvia Hees de Carvalho, Adriana de Souza Andrade, Janaina de Moura Freire, Marco Antônio Campos, Erna Geessien Kroon
Original abstract (English)
Mpox is a zoonotic disease caused by the monkeypox virus, a double-stranded DNA virus belonging to the genus Orthopoxvirus. The World Health Organization (WHO) declared mpox a Public Health Emergency of International Concern (PHEIC) in July 2022 amid the global clade IIb outbreak and again in August 2024 following an increase in cases in the Democratic Republic of the Congo associated with clades Ia and Ib. Despite increased awareness, diagnosing mpox remains challenging because its clinical manifestations often overlap with those of other infectious and non-infectious conditions. This diagnostic challenge occurs across geographic settings but may be further complicated in tropical regions by the circulation of locally prevalent infections, including arboviral diseases such as dengue and chikungunya. Here, we describe a series of nine patients initially evaluated for suspected mpox, in whom alternative diagnoses were subsequently established.

Despite increased awareness, diagnosing mpox remains challenging because its clinical manifestations often overlap with those of other infectious and non-infectious conditions.

Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a case series, which may not capture the full spectrum of mpox presentations. The evidence is moderate as it is based on clinical observations rather than controlled experiments.

Discovery of Mpox Virus in Bile: A Case with Multiple Coinfections.

Authors: Mengze Gan, Jinmei Qin, Xiaoying Qin, Renping Peng, Qiuye Xiao, Shuhui Wang, Yinduo Wei, Liuying Wei, Rui Zhao, Zhouhua Xie
Original abstract (English)
We report the detection of Monkeypox virus (MPXV) nucleic acid in bile from a rash-free patient with advanced HIV and multiple coinfections. This case raises the possibility that MPXV nucleic acid may be detectable in bile and represents, to our knowledge, the first reported detection of MPXV nucleic acid in human bile. The clinical significance of this observation requires further study.

针对猴痘病毒 D8l 和 D4r/A20r 复合物的植物化学候选药物的计算设计:药效团和分子预测的计算机研究

Computational Design of a Phytochemical Drug Candidate Targeting Monkeypox Virus D8l and D4r/A20r Complex: An in Silico Study of Pharmacophoreand Molecular Prediction
作者： Lian Gail Lontiong, Acmilah Batawe, Carmela Plana, Mary Grace Suganob, Mary Antoinette Yap, Mia Joy Inocencio, Carlos Gaygay, Jr, C. Gaygay
摘要中文翻译由于其传播迅速且缺乏批准的 抗病毒药物 治疗,猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 已被宣布为全球卫生紧急事件。本研究旨在确定针对参与 宿主 细胞进入的 MPXV 痘苗蛋白 D8L 和负责病毒 DNA 复制和 宿主 细胞进入的 D4R/A20R 复合物的可能候选药物。使用结合计算筛选、分子对接和蛋白质-配体相互作用分析的计算机定量方法针对 MPXV 靶蛋白评估植物化学和药效团化合物。蛋白质和配体文库获自 RCSB PDB、NCBI BLASTp、PubChem 和 DrugBank。使用 ADMETLab 3.0 评估药物相似性和药代动力学特性。此次筛选将最初的 1,854 种候选植物化学物质和 808 种药效团化合物减少至 168 种候选植物化学物质和 11 种药效团化合物。满足前一个标准的化合物使用 CB-Dock 进行分子对接和空腔评估,然后使用 PLIP 进行相互作用分析。然后使用源自对接等级、氢键和疏水相互作用的标准进一步评估主要配体。在三 (3) 种方法中,Artonol A 和喜树碱表现出相同的强结合亲和力(-9.1 kcal/mol),并且针对各自的蛋白质获得了最高分数。这些发现支持通过体外和体内测试作为治疗 MPXV 的可能选择进行进一步的实验验证。

问题与背景
猴痘病毒(MPXV)由于其快速传播且缺乏批准的 抗病毒药物 治疗,已被宣布为全球卫生紧急事件。

本研究旨在确定针对参与 宿主 细胞进入的 MPXV 痘苗蛋白 D8L 和负责病毒 DNA 复制和 宿主 细胞进入的 D4R/A20R 复合物的可能候选药物。
核心方法

主要结果

解释与创新
这些发现支持通过体外和体内测试作为治疗 MPXV 的可能选择进行进一步的实验验证。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为治疗 MPXV 的新 抗病毒药物 治疗 的开发提供信息,从而可能为管理 猴痘 疾病提供治疗选择。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于计算机方法,需要临床应用的实验验证。证据是温和的,因为它是基于计算模型的。

Computational Design of a Phytochemical Drug Candidate Targeting Monkeypox Virus D8l and D4r/A20r Complex: An in Silico Study of Pharmacophoreand Molecular Prediction

Authors: Lian Gail Lontiong, Acmilah Batawe, Carmela Plana, Mary Grace Suganob, Mary Antoinette Yap, Mia Joy Inocencio, Carlos Gaygay, Jr, C. Gaygay
Original abstract (English)
Due to its rapid spread and the absence of approved antiviral treatments, Monkeypox virus (MPXV) has been declared as a global health emergency. This study aimed to identify possible drug candidates targeting MPXV vaccinia proteins D8L which is involved in host cell entry, and D4R/A20R Complex responsible for viral DNA replication and host cell entry. Phytochemical and pharmacophore compounds were assessed against MPXV target proteins using an in silico quantitative method that combined computational screening, molecular docking, and protein–ligand interaction analysis. Protein and ligand libraries were obtained from RCSB PDB, NCBI BLASTp, PubChem, and DrugBank. Drug-likeness and pharmacokinetic properties were assessed using ADMETLab 3.0. This screening reduced the initial number of 1,854 phytochemical and 808 pharmacophore compounds to 168 phytochemical and 11 pharmacophore candidates. The compounds that satisfied the former criterion were subjected to molecular docking and cavity assessment using CB-Dock which was then followed by an interaction analysis using PLIP. The leading ligands were then further evaluated using a criterion derived from docking rank, hydrogen bonds, and hydrophobic interactions gained. Among the three (3) methods, Artonol A and Camptothecin showed identical strong binding affinities of −9.1 kcal/mol and achieved the highest scores against their respective proteins. These findings support further experimental validation by in vitro and in vivo tests as possible choices for treating MPXV.

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in silico methods, which require experimental validation for clinical application. The evidence is moderate as it is based on computational models.

使用结构引导的命中扩展策略在体外和体内发现核苷类似物 FMAU 作为广谱抗 正痘病毒 抑制剂。

Discovery of the nucleoside analogue FMAU as a broad-spectrum anti-orthopoxvirus inhibitor in vitro and in vivo using a structure-guided hit expansion strategy.
作者： Tingting Zheng, Yong Feng, Chang Xu, Arnaud Fondjo Kouam, Shiman Chen, Farkhod Eshboev, Yingxia Liu, Fuxiang Wang, Yang Yang, Jianxun Qi
摘要中文翻译猴痘 仍然是全球范围内严重的公共卫生威胁,目前针对 正痘病毒 感染的可用疗法仍然非常有限。在这项研究中,我们通过核(酸)类似物库筛选结合结构引导的命中扩展策略,将 2'-脱氧-2'-氟-5-甲基-1-β-D-阿拉伯呋喃糖尿嘧啶 (FMAU) 鉴定为广谱抗 正痘病毒 候选药物。在 Vero 细胞中,FMAU 对痘苗病毒 (VACV) 和猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 发挥有效的体外 抗病毒药物 活性,EC50 值分别为 1.99 μM 和 30 nM,同时没有表现出明显的细胞毒性,如 CC50 值大于 200 μM 所示。小鼠体内研究进一步表明,FMAU 治疗 可针对致命的 VACV 和 MPXV 攻击提供全面保护,同时显着降低肺组织中的病毒载量。重要的是,口服 FMAU 在 MPXV 感染小鼠模型中保留了针对 MPXV 的有效治疗效果。总的来说,我们的研究结果表明 FMAU 是一种有前途的口服抗 正痘病毒 候选药物,并支持其进一步的临床前评估。

Discovery of the nucleoside analogue FMAU as a broad-spectrum anti-orthopoxvirus inhibitor in vitro and in vivo using a structure-guided hit expansion strategy.

Authors: Tingting Zheng, Yong Feng, Chang Xu, Arnaud Fondjo Kouam, Shiman Chen, Farkhod Eshboev, Yingxia Liu, Fuxiang Wang, Yang Yang, Jianxun Qi
Original abstract (English)
Mpox remains a critical public health threat worldwide, and currently available therapeutics against orthopoxvirus infections remain very limited. In this study, we identified 2'-deoxy-2'-fluoro-5-methyl-1-β-D-arabinofuranosyluracil (FMAU) as a broad-spectrum anti-orthopoxvirus candidate through nucleos(t)ide analogue library screening combined with a structure-guided hit expansion strategy. In Vero cells, FMAU exerted potent in vitro antiviral activity against vaccinia virus (VACV) and monkeypox virus (MPXV), with EC50 values of 1.99 μM and 30 nM, respectively, while exhibiting no apparent cytotoxicity, as reflected by a CC50 value greater than 200 μM. In vivo mouse studies further showed that FMAU treatment provided full protection against lethal VACV and MPXV challenges, while significantly reducing viral loads in lung tissues. Importantly, oral FMAU administration retained potent therapeutic efficacy against MPXV in a mouse model of MPXV infection. Collectively, our findings identify FMAU as a promising oral anti-orthopoxvirus drug candidate and support its further preclinical evaluation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In Vero cells, FMAU exerted potent in vitro antiviral activity against vaccinia virus (VACV) and monkeypox virus (MPXV), with EC50 values of 1.99 μM and 30 nM, respectively, while exhibiting no apparent cytotoxicity, as reflected by a CC50 value greater than 200 μM.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods

Main results
In this study, we identified 2'-deoxy-2'-fluoro-5-methyl-1-β-D-arabinofuranosyluracil (FMAU) as a broad-spectrum anti-orthopoxvirus candidate through nucleos(t)ide analogue library screening combined with a structure-guided hit expansion strategy.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

行为意识延迟对肯尼亚 猴痘 离散时间 SIR 模型动态的影响:稳定性和 Neimark-Sacker 分岔分析

Effect of Behavioral Awareness Delay on the Dynamics of a Discrete-Time SIR Model for Mpox in Kenya: Stability and Neimark–Sacker Bifurcation Analysis
作者： Vivian Nyakio Gachie, Iyaya C. C. Wanjala, Joy Mutegi
摘要中文翻译目的/目标:研究延迟行为意识如何影响 猴痘 传播 离散时间 SIR 模型的动态,重点是局部稳定性和 Neimark-Sacker 分叉的发生。研究设计:理论数学建模研究,包括分析稳定性分析和数值模拟。学习地点和期限:肯尼亚多媒体大学科学技术学院;研究于 2025 年 8 月至 2026 年 8 月期间进行。方法论:归一化离散时间 SIR 模型,纳入人口水平行为意识指数 Bn,代表了解 猴痘 感染风险和采取保护行为的组合。在对感染的行为意识反应中引入了离散延迟 t。推导了无病和地方性平衡,并使用线性化和陪审团标准分析了它们的局部稳定性。当 t > 0 时,系统关于地方性平衡进行线性化,并且当 t 变化时,在 MATLAB 中对光谱半径 p 进行数值检查。时间序列模拟和相图用于表征所产生的动态。生成时间序列模拟和相图来说明动态。结果:对于选定的基线参数配置(R0 = 2.07),地方性平衡对于较短的延迟是局部渐近稳定的。随着延迟 t 的增加,一对复共轭特征根以大约 9-10 周的临界延迟穿过单位圆。数值模拟显示持续振荡,与内马克-萨克分岔一致。结论:延迟的行为意识会破坏地方性平衡的稳定,并通过 Neimark-Sacker 分叉诱导振荡 猴痘 动力学。研究结果支持及时行为反应和持续风险沟通的重要性。估计的 9-10 周阈值是所选参数配置下的模型相关结果,不应解释为肯尼亚的经验国家估计。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查延迟行为意识如何影响 猴痘 传播 离散时间 SIR 模型的动态,重点是局部稳定性和 Neimark-Sacker 分岔的发生。
设计与对象
这是 2025 年 8 月至 2026 年 8 月在肯尼亚多媒体大学科学技术学院进行的一项理论数学建模研究。
核心方法
开发了标准化离散时间 SIR 模型,纳入了人群水平行为意识指数 Bn,代表了解 猴痘 感染风险和采取保护行为的组合。在对感染的行为意识反应中引入了离散延迟 t。推导了无病和地方性平衡,并使用线性化和陪审团标准分析了它们的局部稳定性。当 t > 0 时,系统关于地方性平衡进行线性化,并且当 t 变化时,在 MATLAB 中对光谱半径 p 进行数值检查。时间序列模拟和相图用于表征所产生的动态。
主要结果
对于选定的基线参数配置(R0 = 2.07),地方性平衡对于较短的延迟是局部渐近稳定的。随着延迟 t 的增加,一对复共轭特征根以大约 9-10 周的临界延迟穿过单位圆。数值模拟显示持续振荡,与内马克-萨克分岔一致。
解释与创新
作者解释说,延迟的行为意识会破坏地方性平衡的稳定,并通过内马克-萨克尔分叉诱发振荡 猴痘 动力学。这项研究为行为延迟在 猴痘 传播 的稳定性和动态中的作用提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明及时行为反应和持续风险沟通在 猴痘 控制中的重要性。他们认为,延迟认识可能会导致复杂的 传播 动态,这可能对公共卫生策略产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于理论模型,不提供经验数据。估计的 9-10 周阈值是所选参数配置下的模型相关结果,不应解释为肯尼亚的经验国家估计。由于依赖模拟且缺乏真实世界数据,证据并不充分。

Effect of Behavioral Awareness Delay on the Dynamics of a Discrete-Time SIR Model for Mpox in Kenya: Stability and Neimark–Sacker Bifurcation Analysis

Authors: Vivian Nyakio Gachie, Iyaya C. C. Wanjala, Joy Mutegi
Original abstract (English)
Aims/ Objectives: To investigate how delayed behavioral awareness affects the dynamics of a discrete-time SIR model for Mpox transmission, with an emphasis on local stability and the occurrence of a Neimark–Sacker bifurcation. Study Design: Theoretical mathematical modeling study with analytical stability analysis and numerical simulation. Place and Duration of Study: Multimedia University of Kenya, Faculty of Science and Technology; research conducted between August 2025 and August 2026. Methodology: A normalized discrete-time SIR model incorporating a population-level behavioral awareness index Bn representing a combination of being informed about Mpox infection risk and adopting protective behavior. A discrete delay t was introduced in the behavioral-awareness response to the infection. The disease-free and endemic equilibria were derived and their local stability was analyzed using linearization and the Jury criterion. For t > 0, the system was linearized about the endemic equilibrium and the spectral radius p examined numerically in MATLAB as t varied. Time-series simulations and phase portraits were used to characterize the resulting dynamics. Time-series simulations and phase portraits were generated to illustrate the dynamics. Results: For the selected baseline parameter configuration (R0 = 2.07), the endemic equilibrium was locally asymptotically stable for shorter delays. As the delay t increased, a complex-conjugate pair of characteristic roots crossed the unit circle at a critical delay of approximately 9–10 weeks. Numerical simulations showed sustained oscillations, consistent with a Neimark–Sacker bifurcation. Conclusion: Delayed behavioral awareness can destabilize the endemic equilibrium and induce oscillatory Mpox dynamics through a Neimark–Sacker bifurcation. The findings support the importance of timely behavioral response and sustained risk communication. The estimated 9–10 week threshold is a model-dependent result under the selected parameter configuration and should not be interpreted as an empirical national estimate for Kenya.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how delayed behavioral awareness affects the dynamics of a discrete-time SIR model for Mpox transmission, with an emphasis on local stability and the occurrence of a Neimark–Sacker bifurcation.
Design and population
This was a theoretical mathematical modeling study conducted at Multimedia University of Kenya, Faculty of Science and Technology, between August 2025 and August 2026.
Core methods
A normalized discrete-time SIR model was developed, incorporating a population-level behavioral awareness index Bn representing a combination of being informed about Mpox infection risk and adopting protective behavior. A discrete delay t was introduced in the behavioral-awareness response to the infection. The disease-free and endemic equilibria were derived and their local stability was analyzed using linearization and the Jury criterion. For t > 0, the system was linearized about the endemic equilibrium and the spectral radius p examined numerically in MATLAB as t varied. Time-series simulations and phase portraits were used to characterize the resulting dynamics.
Main results
For the selected baseline parameter configuration (R0 = 2.07), the endemic equilibrium was locally asymptotically stable for shorter delays. As the delay t increased, a complex-conjugate pair of characteristic roots crossed the unit circle at a critical delay of approximately 9–10 weeks. Numerical simulations showed sustained oscillations, consistent with a Neimark–Sacker bifurcation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that delayed behavioral awareness can destabilize the endemic equilibrium and induce oscillatory Mpox dynamics through a Neimark–Sacker bifurcation. This study provides novel insights into the role of behavioral delay in the stability and dynamics of Mpox transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of timely behavioral response and sustained risk communication in Mpox control. They suggest that delayed awareness may lead to complex transmission dynamics, which could have implications for public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on theoretical modeling and does not provide empirical data. The estimated 9–10 week threshold is a model-dependent result under the selected parameter configuration and should not be interpreted as an empirical national estimate for Kenya. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on simulation and the lack of real-world data.

根据国家统一健康目标评估美国联邦人畜共患记录:针对 COVID-19、猴痘和 H5N1 的多法学硕士评估

Evaluating U.S. Federal Zoonotic Records Against National One Health Goals: A Multi-LLM Assessment Across COVID-19, Monkeypox, and H5N1
作者： Bowen Long, Fangya Tan, Jinghang Zhang
摘要中文翻译(1) 背景:人畜共患疾病需要人类、动物和环境卫生部门采取协调行动。虽然大量研究集中在 暴发 预测、预测和 监测 上,但很少关注系统评估政府政策文件中如何体现“同一个健康”原则。(2) 方法:我们开发了一个多大语言模型 (LLM) 框架来评估 477 份美国政府文件,涵盖 COVID-19、猴痘和高致病性禽流感 (H5N1)。机构技术指南和联邦公报文件使用涵盖美国国家统一健康框架的三个目标的 19 项标题进行评估:协调和沟通、暴发 响应和 监测。Claude、GPT 和 Gemini 独立评估了这些文件。评估了模型间一致性和人工验证,敏感性分析检查了替代的块到文档聚合阈值和文档长度的潜在影响。(3)结果:1健康一致性普遍有限,没有疾病×文献类型×目标组合超过39%。文件类型显示出最明显的差异,机构技术指南显示这三个目标的一致性比《联邦公报》文件高出约 13-31 个百分点,而不同疾病之间的差异则不太一致。在按长度限制 B 类文档之后,敏感性分析支持跨替代聚合阈值的主要文档类型模式。在排除一项措辞敏感的标题项目后,三位法学硕士在 87.1% 的文档项目评估中达成了一致,其中人与模型的一致性(科恩的 κ = 0.513-0.692)与人与人的一致性(κ = 0.649)相当。(4) 结论:本研究引入了专门构建的政策语料库和结构化的多法学硕士评估框架,用于研究“同一个健康”原则如何在政府文件中得到体现。最明显的差异是在文档类型而不是疾病之间观察到的,并且主要发现在不同的分析假设中总体上是稳定的。总之,这些发现将人畜共患疾病研究扩展到 暴发 预测和 监测 之外,转向对 One Health 政策一致性的系统评估。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人畜共患疾病需要人类、动物和环境卫生部门采取协调行动。虽然大量研究集中在 暴发 预测、预测和 监测 上,但很少关注系统评估政府政策文件中如何体现“同一个健康”原则。
设计与对象
我们开发了一个多大型语言模型 (LLM) 框架来评估 477 份涉及 COVID-19、猴痘和高致病性禽流感 (H5N1) 的美国政府文件。机构技术指南和联邦公报文件使用涵盖美国国家统一健康框架的三个目标的 19 项标题进行评估:协调和沟通、暴发 响应和 监测。
核心方法
Claude、GPT 和 Gemini 独立评估了这些文件。评估了模型间一致性和人工验证,敏感性分析检查了替代的块到文档聚合阈值和文档长度的潜在影响。
主要结果
1 健康一致性普遍有限,没有疾病×文档类型×目标组合超过39%。文件类型显示出最明显的差异,机构技术指南显示这三个目标的一致性比《联邦公报》文件高出约 13-31 个百分点,而不同疾病之间的差异则不太一致。
解释与创新
本研究引入了专门构建的政策语料库和结构化的多法学硕士评估框架,用于检查政府文件中如何体现“同一个健康”原则。最明显的差异是在文档类型而不是疾病之间观察到的,并且主要发现在不同的分析假设中总体上是稳定的。
科研与公卫意义
总之,这些发现将人畜共患疾病研究扩展到 暴发 预测和 监测 之外,转向对 One Health 政策一致性的系统评估。该研究深入探讨了如何构建政策文件,以更好地反映人畜共患疾病背景下的“同一个健康”原则。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告与 猴痘病毒 评估相关的具体限制。该研究的结果基于仅抽象的分析,由于缺乏 猴痘病毒 本身的详细数据,证据强度中等。

Evaluating U.S. Federal Zoonotic Records Against National One Health Goals: A Multi-LLM Assessment Across COVID-19, Monkeypox, and H5N1

Authors: Bowen Long, Fangya Tan, Jinghang Zhang
Original abstract (English)
(1) Background: Zoonotic diseases require coordinated action across human, animal, and environmental health sectors. While substantial research has focused on outbreak prediction, forecasting, and surveillance, less attention has been given to systematically evaluating how One Health principles are reflected in government policy documents. (2) Methods: We developed a multi-large language model (LLM) framework to evaluate 477 U.S. government documents covering COVID-19, monkeypox, and highly pathogenic avian influenza (H5N1). Agency technical guidance and Federal Register documents were evaluated using a 19-item rubric covering three goals of the U.S. National One Health Framework: coordination and communication, outbreak response, and surveillance. Claude, GPT, and Gemini independently evaluated the documents. Inter-model agreement and human validation were assessed, and sensitivity analyses examined alternative chunk-to-document aggregation thresholds and the potential influence of document length. (3) Results: One Health alignment was generally limited, with no disease × document-type × goal combination exceeding 39%. Document type showed the clearest differences, with agency technical guidance showing approximately 13–31 percentage-point higher alignment than Federal Register documents across the three goals, while differences across diseases were less consistent. Sensitivity analyses supported the main document-type pattern across alternative aggregation thresholds and after restricting Type B documents by length. After excluding one wording-sensitive rubric item, the three LLMs reached unanimous agreement in 87.1% of document-item evaluations, with human–model agreement (Cohen’s κ = 0.513–0.692) comparable to human–human agreement (κ = 0.649). (4) Conclusions: This study introduces a purpose-built policy corpus and structured multi-LLM evaluation framework for examining how One Health principles are reflected in government documents. The clearest differences were observed across document types rather than diseases, and the main findings were generally stable across alternative analytical assumptions. Together, these findings extend zoonotic-disease research beyond outbreak prediction and surveillance toward systematic evaluation of One Health policy alignment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Zoonotic diseases require coordinated action across human, animal, and environmental health sectors. While substantial research has focused on outbreak prediction, forecasting, and surveillance, less attention has been given to systematically evaluating how One Health principles are reflected in government policy documents.
Design and population
We developed a multi-large language model (LLM) framework to evaluate 477 U.S. government documents covering COVID-19, monkeypox, and highly pathogenic avian influenza (H5N1). Agency technical guidance and Federal Register documents were evaluated using a 19-item rubric covering three goals of the U.S. National One Health Framework: coordination and communication, outbreak response, and surveillance.
Core methods
Claude, GPT, and Gemini independently evaluated the documents. Inter-model agreement and human validation were assessed, and sensitivity analyses examined alternative chunk-to-document aggregation thresholds and the potential influence of document length.
Main results
One Health alignment was generally limited, with no disease × document-type × goal combination exceeding 39%. Document type showed the clearest differences, with agency technical guidance showing approximately 13–31 percentage-point higher alignment than Federal Register documents across the three goals, while differences across diseases were less consistent.
Interpretation and novelty
This study introduces a purpose-built policy corpus and structured multi-LLM evaluation framework for examining how One Health principles are reflected in government documents. The clearest differences were observed across document types rather than diseases, and the main findings were generally stable across alternative analytical assumptions.
Scientific and public-health significance
Together, these findings extend zoonotic-disease research beyond outbreak prediction and surveillance toward systematic evaluation of One Health policy alignment. The study provides insights into how policy documents can be structured to better reflect One Health principles in the context of zoonotic diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations related to the evaluation of the mpox virus. The study's findings are based on an abstract-only analysis, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed data on the mpox virus itself.

HECA-YOLOv11:一种高精度混合注意力网络,用于实时猴痘皮肤病变检测并具有可解释性

HECA-YOLOv11: A High-Accuracy Hybrid Attention Network for Real-Time Monkeypox Skin Lesion Detection with Interpretability
作者： Xianghong Deng, Chonlatee Photong
摘要中文翻译简介:全球 暴发 猴痘风险需要高效的诊断工具。目前的方法速度缓慢,并且难以区分相似的皮肤病变,而深度学习模型缺乏多类分类的准确性和可解释性。本研究旨在开发一种高性能、实时筛查工具,用于早期检测猴痘和类似病症。方法:本研究提出了一种增强的架构,将高效通道注意力(HECA)机制纳入 YOLOv11 中,以改进特征选择。HECA 对输入特征图执行全局平均池化 (GAP) 和最大平均池化 (MAP)。该研究利用空间变换和像素域增强,建立并增强了包含猴痘、麻疹、水痘和正常皮肤图像的四类数据集。梯度加权类激活映射 (Grad-CAM) 生成用于病变定位可视化的热图,而基于 PyQt5 的交互式诊断界面则简化了临床工作流程。结果:实验结果表明,与测试集上的现有方法(EfficientNet-v2-l、GoogLeNet、ResNet50、VGG19、MobileNetv2、DenseNet201 和 YOLOv11s)相比,所提出的模型具有优越的性能,具有增强的检测速度、准确度(98.9%)、精确度(99%)、召回率(99.3%)和 F1 分数(99.1%)。在所有评估指标中,HECA-YOLOv11 的性能均优于 SE-YOLOv11 和 ECA-YOLOv11 超过 5.1%。热图分析证实了该模型对病变区域的关注,与医学推理相一致。讨论:集成的注意力机制和可解释性技术有效地解决了自动皮肤病变检测的关键限制。高性能和低延迟标志着可靠临床决策支持的重大进步,对早期 暴发 遏制具有重要意义。结论:HECA-YOLOv11框架为早期猴痘筛查提供了高度准确和高效的工具。其标准化界面在加速公共卫生应对和改善新发传染病的全球控制方面具有巨大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了公共卫生对快速、准确的诊断工具的迫切需求,以减轻 猴痘 的全球 暴发 风险,强调现有方法速度缓慢,而且深度学习模型缺乏多类皮肤病变分类的准确性和可解释性。
设计与对象
作者开发并评估了一个深度学习框架 (HECA-YOLOv11),旨在使用四类图像数据集实时筛查 猴痘 和类似的皮肤病变。
核心方法
该架构将高效通道注意力(HECA)机制集成到YOLOv11中,将全局平均池化和最大平均池化应用于输入特征图,并使用空间变换和像素域增强来增强包含猴痘、麻疹、水痘和正常皮肤图像的数据集。
主要结果
在测试集上,HECA-YOLOv11 实现了 98.9% 的准确率、99% 的精确率、99.3% 的召回率和 99.1% 的 F1 分数,优于 EfficientNet-v2-l、GoogLeNet、ResNet50、VGG19、MobileNetv2、DenseNet201、YOLOv11s、SE-YOLOv11 和 ECA-YOLOv11所有指标均为 5.1%。
解释与创新
作者将集成注意力机制和可解释性技术解释为有效解决自动皮肤病变检测的关键限制,标志着早期 猴痘 筛查的可靠临床决策支持的重大进步。
科研与公卫意义
HECA-YOLOv11 的标准化界面和高性能表明有可能通过早期检测加速公共卫生反应并改善对新发传染病的全球控制。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告数据集规模、地理或人群范围、外部验证或前瞻性临床评估,限制了结论的普遍性,且证据强度为中等。

HECA-YOLOv11: A High-Accuracy Hybrid Attention Network for Real-Time Monkeypox Skin Lesion Detection with Interpretability

Authors: Xianghong Deng, Chonlatee Photong
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: The global outbreak risk of monkeypox necessitates efficient diagnostic tools. Current methods are slow and struggle to differentiate similar skin lesions, while deep learning models lack both accuracy and interpretability for multi-class classification. This study aims to develop a high-performance, real-time screening tool for early detection of monkeypox and similar conditions. Methods: This study proposes an enhanced architecture that incorporates the Highly Efficient Channel Attention (HECA) mechanism into YOLOv11 to improve feature selection. HECA performs Global Average Pooling (GAP) and Max Average Pooling (MAP) on input feature maps. Using spatial transformations and pixel-domain enhancements, the research establishes and augments a four-class dataset containing monkeypox, measles, chickenpox, and normal skin images. Gradient-Weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM) generates heatmaps for lesion localization visualization, while a PyQt5-based interactive diagnostic interface streamlines clinical workflows. Results: Experimental results demonstrate the proposed model’s superior performance compared to existing approaches (EfficientNet-v2-l, GoogLeNet, ResNet50, VGG19, MobileNetv2, DenseNet201, and YOLOv11s) on the test set, with enhanced detection speed, accuracy (98.9%), precision (99%), recall (99.3%), and F1-score (99.1%). HECA-YOLOv11 outperforms SE-YOLOv11 and ECA-YOLOv11 by over 5.1% across all evaluation metrics. Heatmap analysis confirms the model’s focus on lesion regions, aligning with medical reasoning. Discussion: The integrated attention mechanism and interpretability techniques effectively address key limitations in automated skin lesion detection. The high performance and low latency signify a major advancement towards reliable clinical decision support, with important implications for early outbreak containment. Conclusion: The HECA-YOLOv11 framework provides a highly accurate and efficient tool for early monkeypox screening. Its standardized interface holds significant potential to accelerate public health responses and improve global control of emerging infectious diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the urgent public-health need for rapid, accurate diagnostic tools to mitigate the global outbreak risk of mpox, highlighting that existing methods are slow and deep learning models lack both accuracy and interpretability for multi-class skin lesion classification.
Design and population
The authors developed and evaluated a deep learning framework (HECA-YOLOv11) designed for real-time screening of mpox and similar skin lesions using a four-class image dataset.
Core methods
The proposed architecture integrates a Highly Efficient Channel Attention (HECA) mechanism into YOLOv11, applying Global Average Pooling and Max Average Pooling to input feature maps, and augments a dataset containing monkeypox, measles, chickenpox, and normal skin images using spatial transformations and pixel-domain enhancements.
Main results
On the test set, HECA-YOLOv11 achieved 98.9% accuracy, 99% precision, 99.3% recall, and 99.1% F1-score, outperforming EfficientNet-v2-l, GoogLeNet, ResNet50, VGG19, MobileNetv2, DenseNet201, YOLOv11s, SE-YOLOv11, and ECA-YOLOv11 by over 5.1% across all metrics.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the integrated attention mechanism and interpretability techniques as effectively addressing key limitations in automated skin lesion detection, marking a major advancement toward reliable clinical decision support for early mpox screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
The standardized interface and high performance of HECA-YOLOv11 suggest potential to accelerate public health responses and improve global control of emerging infectious diseases through early detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report dataset size, geographic or demographic scope, external validation, or prospective clinical evaluation, limiting generalisability and leaving the strength of evidence moderate.
